Each card is three stacked instances (Default with hover to the Hover variant, Dimmed, Selected) whose visibility is bound to dim-i and sel-i; a click on a card sets all sel and dim variables, chosen true, notchosen false, choose-error false. Dimmed cards light up on hover and can be clicked to change the choice. The confirm button is two instances: Disabled (visible while notchosen; click sets choose-error true and shows "Choose one to continue.") and enabled (visible while chosen; click or Enter goes to 4d, a transition frame with the button in Loading that moves to the Welcome frame after 1.4 s). "Skip for now" goes straight to the Welcome frame. Static review frames: 4b Choice selected (17026:4789), 4c Nothing chosen error (17026:5028), 4d Confirming (17026:560, live variables).
- Welcome (17015:398): the button now reads "Go to Community". It still returns to the landing frame 16781:941 as a placeholder because Community is not designed. No other Home text remains in the join section.
- Steps 1 and 2 are now state machines across frames (the sign-in panel convention: clicking a field simulates typing, Continue walks through the states): 1a Email or mobile, 1b Focus, 1c Missing ("Enter your email or mobile number."), 1d Invalid entry, 1e Already registered (message "That email already has an account." plus a "Log in instead" link that opens the sign-in panel), 1f Sending (button Loading, goes to 2a after 1.5 s), 1g Connecting to Google and 1h Connecting to Apple (Loading provider button, go to step 3 after 1.8 s). Step 2: 2a Confirm code, 2b Focus, 2c Missing ("Enter the 6-digit code."), 2d Wrong code, 2e Expired code ("That code has expired. Send a new code."), 2f Checking (goes to 3a after 1.6 s), 2g New code sent (green notice). Click the field to go to Focus; Continue (click or Enter) goes 1a to 1c to 1d to 1e to 1f to 2a, and 2a to 2c to 2d to 2e to 2f to 3a; "Send a new code" goes to 2g; "Use a different email" goes back to 1a and resets all variables. The happy path therefore needs repeated Continue clicks; Google and Apple are the quick route to step 3.
- Step 3 is one live frame (17029:162, "3a Create account (interactive)") driven by variables: Username layers u-empty, u-focus, u-invalid, u-taken (with three suggestion chips), u-ok, u-missing; Password layers p-empty, p-focus, p-weak-masked, p-weak-shown, p-strong-masked, p-strong-shown, p-missing (the Show and Hide words are click targets that swap masked and shown); Tick layers k-off, k-on, k-error. Clicking Username steps empty to focus to invalid to taken, and a chip or another click gives available; clicking Password steps empty to focus to weak to strong; clicking the tick toggles it. "Create my account" (click or Enter) uses Figma conditional actions: empty username or password becomes the missing error, an unticked box becomes the tick error, and when username is available, password is strong and the box is ticked it resets the step 4 variables and goes to 3e (button Loading, goes to 4a after 1.5 s). Static review frames: 3b All fields missing (17030:421), 3c Username taken (17030:629), 3d Ready to create (17030:809), 3f Invalid username and weak password (17030:999). A weak password does not block submit with an error of its own; it only shows the meter.
- Resets: every landing "CTA / Join Akines" and "CTA / Join the community" button (three Desktop landing frames), every "Create an account" click target and "Use a different email" set all 37 join variables back to their defaults before going to 1a, so the flow always starts clean.
- Layout: section 07 is now a 4 column grid in step order (rows: 1a to 1d, 1e to 1h, 2a to 2d, 2e to 2g, 3a to 3d, 3e and 3f, 4a to 4d, Welcome), 6736 x 9024 at x 38800, y -1578, plus an Annotations frame explaining how to play it. Navigation graph checked by script: every state frame has an incoming path except the static reference frames (3b, 3c, 3d, 3f, 4b, 4c), nothing points to a missing frame. Not played in Present mode from the workspace.
- Limits: the Show toggle on the sign-in panel itself is still not interactive; a weak password does not get its own error on submit; clicking the "Create an account" panel link on a landing frame uses an invisible click frame (the panel is a component on another page); tablet and mobile join frames and tablet and mobile sign-in panels are still to do after desktop approval.
- Tooling notes: (50) figma.createFrame clones land on the page root, not in the original's section; append them to the section before wiring NAVIGATE. (51) Custom properties cannot be set on node objects; keep side maps in plain JS objects. (52) Conditional actions work through setReactionsAsync: {type:'CONDITIONAL', conditionalBlocks:[{condition:{type:'EXPRESSION',resolvedType:'BOOLEAN',value:{expressionFunction:'AND'|'OR',expressionArguments:[{type:'VARIABLE_ALIAS',resolvedType:'BOOLEAN',value:{type:'VARIABLE_ALIAS',id}}]}},actions:[...]}]}. (53) Bound paints lose their own opacity, so use plain paints when opacity is needed (the hover ring and index text). (54) A variant swap keeps instance overrides (image fill, text), so one photo override covers all four Radio Card states. (55) Static review frames are made by cloning a variable-driven frame, setting visible by hand on every node bound to the join variables, then unbinding and clearing reactions.

- Tooling notes: (65) setProperties on a nested instance-swap property creates a new nested instance and resets its visibility. (66) Instance-level reactions on nested instances inside a component can carry both a hover CHANGE_TO (to the nested set's variant) and a click CHANGE_TO (to the outer set's variant); a self-targeting CHANGE_TO is rejected. (67) resize() resets hug sizing to FIXED, so set primaryAxisSizingMode AUTO again afterwards. (68) createFrame() clips content by default, which hides absolutely positioned tooltips; set clipsContent false on rows and component sets. (69) "Desktop" (12001:8778), "Tablet" and "Mobile" under V2 are separate PAGES, not sections; flow starting points are stored on each of them.

- Tooling notes: (72) Variant names are case sensitive ("Selected=True"), build lookups from the variant names and not from booleans. (73) Reactions on nodes nested two levels deep inside an instance (demo variant, Bar instance, Item instance) are accepted and replace the inherited ones, so include the press reaction again when overriding. (74) A sheet that slides in from below must start outside the frame by more than its shadow (here 80 px) or the shadow shows at the bottom of the closed state. (75) Component sets keep manual child positions after combineAsVariants; set x and y per variant then call resizeWithoutConstraints on the set to rearrange variants into a grid. (76) Put a press reaction on the specific node (avatar button), not on the variant root, or pressing anywhere highlights it. (78) Absolutely positioned children inside a component that will be stretched (FILL items) need CENTER or STRETCH constraints, not the default MIN, or they drift when the instance is wider than the component; instances re-flow by themselves after the constraint change. (79) In this environment findOne on an instance skips hidden layers, so read instance.children directly when a layer may be hidden. (77) Use BACKGROUND_BLUR effect plus an unbound semi-transparent fill for the backdrop; Smart Animate interpolates its opacity.

Variables: collection "Community Home state" (VariableCollectionId:17086:149), all BOOLEAN: int-1..8, c-v/c-d/c-empty (continue), p-* (introductions: view, dismiss, act, sent, more, link, empty), i-* (invitations: view, dismiss, pending, accepted, empty), u-rem/u-set (upcoming reminders), w-more/w-link, m-none/good/okay/low/support (mood), time slots "<way>-t1..t3", mt-1..3, ec-1..3, r-form/r-saved, b-join/b-joined, z-join/z-joined, msg-sent*, msg-new, s-saved1..4 (settings saved), pv-1..3 (privacy invite choice).
Wiring: 605 registered interactive nodes, 597 reactions applied (8 skipped because they would navigate to their own frame, for example the active header item), zero errors. Header on every screen: logo and Community Home to Home, Connect, Collaborate, Make & Sell, Explore, Care to their placeholders, Messages to the inbox, Notifications to the popover. Every overlay has a scrim that closes it and its own close controls; navigating from an overlay closes it. Reachability check: all 50 non-state frames are reachable from Home; Loading (auto-returns to Home after 1.5 s), Section error (Try again goes to Loading) and Empty are state boards reached from their own starting points. Audit: 360 interactive kit instances checked, the only ones without reactions are the four active settings nav items (intentional).
Library approach (so another tool can continue): helper source is stored in figma.root shared plugin data, namespace "akines.lib", keys src, src2, src3, src4 (builders), icons (name to component id, 63 icons), imgs (photo hashes), kit (set ids), dest (names to frame ids), wire.<tag> (registry arrays of {n: nodeId, t: trigger, a: specs}). Load with `new AsyncFunction('figma','return ('+getSharedPluginData('akines.lib','src')+')(figma);')`, then src2 to src4 with (figma, L). Specs are ['nav',dest], ['overlay',dest], ['swap',dest], ['close'], ['setv',var,bool], ['when',[vars],[specs]].
Limits and honest gaps: (1) avatar menu items are not individually prototyped (component-level reactions cannot point across pages and nested reactions are lost on a variant swap). (2) Connect, Collaborate, Make & Sell, Explore and Care are placeholders; their header tabs are not wired. (3) Overlay position and backdrop settings are read-only in the Plugin API, so each overlay is a full-size frame with its own scrim and a positioned panel; in Present mode check that overlays sit centred on the viewport. (4) The Error and Empty states are static boards built from the live section clones, not generated by the live Home page. (5) Not play-tested in Present mode from the workspace: screenshots were checked, reactions were counted and destinations validated. (6) Tablet and Mobile Community Home not designed. (7) The 12-item PRD navigation was deliberately not used.
Tooling notes: (80) L.frame pad arrays are [left, top, right, bottom]; passing [232,0,96,0] gave left 232, not top 232. (81) SET_VARIABLE, CONDITIONAL and the exclusive-choice "chipGroup" (idle copy plus an absolute Selected copy bound to a variable) were used to make single choices without extra components. (82) Variable-bound `visible` hides a layer from layout, so hidden states take no space in the Home height. (83) A clone of a section keeps its variable bindings; remove them with `setBoundVariable('visible', null)` before showing a hidden wrapper. (84) get_screenshot with enableBase64Response true works when the image URL cannot be downloaded from the sandbox; the Figma proxy drops often on big frames, retry at a smaller maxDimension. (85) `Header Icon Button` is a COMPONENT_SET, use defaultVariant for createInstance.

### Logged-in links into Community Home, 1 Nov 2026 (14:20 request, done by about 14:45 Lagos)
User request: "Link necessary pages to the Community home, but only a login user." Rule applied: only frames a logged-in member reaches may link to Home (17095:5812). Links into Home from outside section 10:
- 08 JOIN, "5 Welcome": "Go to Community" already led to Home (kept).
- 07 SIGN-IN PANEL (changed): the sign-in demo used to swap variants in place, so it could never leave the panel (component variants on the Components page cannot navigate to frames on another page). It is now a chain of top-level boards using instance-level overrides: 1 Default to 2 Focus (tap an input) or 3 Missing (Log in), 3 to 4 Invalid (Log in), 4 to 5 Loading (Log in), 5 to 6 Success after 1.8 s, and 6 goes to Community Home after 1.5 s (dissolve 0.3). Inputs on 3 and 4 return to 2; the Enter key advances like Log in on 2, 3 and 4; "Use a different account" on 6 returns to 1. Visuals are unchanged (the boards show the same states as before). Not changed: Close and Backdrop (still swap in place), Google and Apple buttons, hover states.
- 09 HEADER (SIGNED IN): in all four demo frames the Logo and "Nav / Community Home" now navigate to the real Home (before, they swapped the header variant in place). Other nav items and tabs in these demo frames still swap in place.
Not done: Tablet and Mobile have no Community Home yet, so their sign-in, join and header frames are not linked; Log out and the avatar menu items are still unlinked; the Success board still shows the reserved "Next step" box for 1.5 s before going Home (replace it when the second authentication step is designed).
Tooling note: (86) A component variant cannot hold a NAVIGATE to a frame on another page (error: destination must be a different top-level frame on the same page). Instance-level overrides on the instance root and on nested nodes are accepted and persist while that variant is shown; they are lost when the instance swaps variant, so chain separate frames instead of swapping in place.

Tooling notes: (87) Underline with `setRangeTextDecoration(0, len, 'UNDERLINE')`, not an `under` flag in L.text; text linked to a component property shares its formatting across all variants, so use a separate hover rule layer instead of a hover-only underline. (88) A direct text child painted with L.P can render black: rebind with `figma.variables.setBoundVariableForPaint({type:'SOLID', color: base}, 'color', variable)` and an explicit base colour (Static/White variable 16248:5186, Text/Primary base 0.0941). (89) `swapComponent` keeps the node id, text overrides, click reactions and variable-bound visibility, but instances without overrides take the new master defaults; after swapComponent or setProperties({Style}) rebuild the reactions as ON_HOVER CHANGE_TO new hover, ON_PRESS CHANGE_TO new pressed, then the old ON_CLICK. (90) A hidden node exposes no children: unbind `visible` (`setBoundVariable('visible', null)`), show it, edit, hide it, rebind the variable. (91) Progressive blur: rectangle with black fill at 0.01 opacity inside a clipped absolute frame with effect `{type:'BACKGROUND_BLUR', blurType:'PROGRESSIVE', radius, startRadius:0, startOffset:{x:.5,y:0}, endOffset:{x:.5,y:1}, visible:true}`, plus a gradient Scrim rectangle (`gradientTransform [[0,1,0],[-1,0,1]]`, stops alpha 0, .42 at .5, .85 at 1, colour 0.05, 0.04, 0.03). (92) `resize()` resets sizing to FIXED, so set FILL or HUG after appending to the auto-layout parent; wrap rows use `counterAxisSpacing`. (93) Top-level state frames are fixed height: after editing their Page, `resize(1512, page.height)`. A clone of a Home band brings its reactions and variable bindings with it, so build state frames by cloning and then removing or hiding parts.
Not done / open: collab-chi 17106:7625, collab-zine 17106:7791 and banter 17106:7962 still use the earlier button styles (could adopt Card Link and the Light pill); Reflect, support and quiet screens have no photos; modals are still radius 32; Tablet and Mobile Community Home wait for approval.

### Carousels for Ways to connect and Active collaborations, 1 Nov 2026 (15:35 to 16:10 Lagos)
Requests: (15:35, with a Dior "The finishing touch" screenshot) turn Ways to connect into that layout: short copy on the left, tall photo cards on the right, a time based carousel of 5 seconds, then the user can use navigation buttons; minimal text like the reference. (15:36, with a screenshot of band 07) turn Active collaborations into one row of 3 cards, also as a carousel.
Mechanism (both): a component set with Page=1..N variants, built on the Desktop page inside section 10 at x 500 (not on the Components page) because a variant reaction can only point at frames on its own page, and the cards must open the overlays and screens of Community Home. Each variant has the same layers (Viewport clipped, Track that holds the cards, Controls with Previous, Counter text, Next); only Track x and the counter text change, so CHANGE_TO with Smart Animate (0.6 s, ease in and out) slides the cards. Variant root reaction: AFTER_TIMEOUT 5 s to the next page (last page wraps to page 1). Previous and Next instances: hover, press and click (click changes the page, both wrap). Nested card buttons carry their own hover, press and click, so a card keeps working after any slide. The Home instances ("Ways carousel", "Collaborations carousel") inherit all of it.

Controls "‹ 1 / 4 ›" bottom right. 7 ways, 2 per page (last page shows only Support Call): Quick Catch-up (overlay w-quick), Talk Something Through (w-talk), Create Together (w-create), Quiet Company (screen quiet 17106:8135), Circle Call (w-circle), Play Call (w-play), Support Call (w-support). Card = photo 440 x 330, then collaborator avatars and names, project name, two line description (fixed 52 high so the buttons align), activity, last activity, primary "Continue collaborating" and an underlined Card Link. Cards: Community Health Initiative (collab-chi 17106:7625, overlay cc-amara), Lagos Makers Zine (collab-zine 17106:7791, cc-zine), and two sample collaborations added by me because a carousel of 3 needs at least 4 cards: Food Systems Cliq Plan (opens the Cliq thread 17105:7345, link opens w-create) and Game Night Planning (university friends thread 17105:7746, link "Start a Play Call" opens w-play). Tooling notes: (94) Component sets that must navigate to frames can live on the destination page inside the flow section; build variants with `figma.createComponent()`, append to the section, then `combineAsVariants(components, section)` and place the set with x and y. (95) Nested instance reactions inside variants accept CHANGE_TO to the parent set's variants and to their own set (include hover and press again, because setting reactions replaces inherited ones). (96) Do not clone from nodes you are about to delete: a template reaction was read from a removed card and failed (read it from a surviving node). (97) A carousel slide needs identical layer names across variants; only change position. (98) get_screenshot drops often at 1000 px or more, retry at 900.
Open: autoplay and slide timing were not play tested in Present mode (Figma only runs AFTER_TIMEOUT on component variants while the frame is on screen); the last page of Ways has one card and a blank area; two collaboration cards are sample content.

Tooling notes: (102) My F() helper must set primaryAxisSizingMode and counterAxisSizingMode to AUTO, otherwise a horizontal frame keeps the default 100 px height. (103) `appendChild` returns nothing, do not chain on it. (104) A frame that holds an ABSOLUTE child must itself be an auto layout frame (set layoutMode first). (105) combineAsVariants leaves all variants at the same x, y: lay them out yourself and resize the set. (106) Card Link frames detached from an instance keep Arrow hidden when the Arrow property is false: set the nested Arrow instance visible. (107) SCROLL_TO needs transition null (a SMART_ANIMATE transition was rejected as invalid reaction), the page frame needs overflowDirection VERTICAL.

Tooling notes: (111) Resizing a child of an instance (for example a rectangle used as a progress bar) silently does nothing in the plugin API: use a fill or visibility override instead, for example five segment rectangles with fills overridden. (113) createComponentFromNode needs the frame to be on the page first (append it to the holder frame), then combineAsVariants with the holder as parent; lay variants out by hand and resizeWithoutConstraints the set. (114) Negative itemSpacing works for overlapping avatars. (115) After widening a flow section, re-flow the following sections (x = previous x + width + 1000) and check overlaps for the whole page.

Tooling notes: (119) `layoutSizingHorizontal = 'FIXED'` then `resize` is needed to give a heading frame a fixed width inside a SPACE_BETWEEN row, otherwise it collapses and the headline wraps per word. (120) Overlays must be full-viewport frames (1512 x 982) with a Scrim rectangle (ABSOLUTE, 0.001 opacity fill) that carries the CLOSE reaction; the dim is the frame fill at 35 percent plus a BACKGROUND_BLUR effect. (121) Reactions read from an instance include the inherited component ones: copy them (JSON round trip, keep trigger and actions only), drop the same trigger type and append your ON_CLICK, otherwise hover and press are lost. (122) A reaction with several actions (NAVIGATE then OVERLAY, CHANGE_TO then OVERLAY) is accepted. (123) Instance children such as chat messages keep a fixed width after createInstance: set layoutSizingHorizontal FILL when they sit in a narrower parent. (124) Header shell tabs carry a CHANGE_TO shell variant click reaction: override only ON_CLICK with NAVIGATE per screen. (125) createSection then set fills, strokes (bound with setBoundVariableForPaint), strokeWeight 40, strokeAlign INSIDE, cornerRadius 80 works on sections; reparent frames with appendChild and then set x and y relative to the section.

### Flow sections rearranged into one section per feature with one column per journey, V2 Desktop, Tablet and Mobile, 2 Nov 2026 (11:04 request, done by about 11:25 Lagos), awaiting approval
User request (2 Nov 11:04, /figma-flow-sections with the updated skill): "Rearrange the flow sections in these V2 pages: 1. Desktop 2. Tablet 3. Mobile". The 1 Nov request said "Without breaking prototype" and I treated that as still in force. This replaces the 1 Nov layout (the first version of the journey grid, which had one wide grid per section).
What changed: x and y of screens inside their own section, the Flow Titles (one per journey, cloned from the existing one), frame names (path style), section names and sizes, section x (1000 px apart, y 0) and the flow starting points. No screen was re-parented between sections except one move on Tablet (see below), resized, rebuilt or deleted, and no reaction was edited by me.
Rules applied (updated skill): journeys are columns and steps are rows; the first screen sits 300 px under its Flow Title and the last screen of the column is the outcome; variants of a step stack down the same column; modals, toasts and overlays sit one column to the right of their trigger (several stack downward); a tall page sits alone in the first row with its states beside it. 500 px margins, Flow Title at (500, 500), 300 px between screens and rows, sections 1000 px apart. Mobile Flow Titles are 1180 wide. The notes frame (Annotations) sits to the right of the title band at the top, so it never sets a column width. Plain page families (Desktop 02, 03, 04, 05, 09 and Mobile 02) keep the legacy strip (152 px under the title, 300 px across, 200 px between rows) and their names.
Frame names: `NN.J - Feature / Journey / Step`. The first screen carries the journey name only, overlays are `Modal:Name` or `Toast:Name`, PRD codes (A1 to A8, B1 to B12) and Join codes (1a, 2b ...) stay in the step name. Flow Title in multi-journey sections: S/N `NN.J`, Main Title = journey name, Name Flow = the old Name Flow + " / " + feature. No START or END markers were added.
Same section numbers, order and names on all three pages. Desktop: 01 EDITORIAL LANDING, 02 SEE HOW IT WORKS, 03 MATCHING AND TRUST, 04 SCROLLED HEADER, 05 SCROLL PROTOTYPE, 06 MENU, 07 SIGN-IN PANEL, 08 JOIN (CREATE ACCOUNT), 09 HEADER (SIGNED IN), 10 COMMUNITY HOME, 11 CONNECT, 12 COLLABORATE, 13 MAKE & SELL, 14 EXPLORE, 15 CARE. Tablet now has 01, 06, 07, 08 and Mobile 01, 02, 06, 07, 08 (they were numbered 01 to 05 before).
Journeys per section:
- 06 MENU: the Context preview frame in column 1, the Menu modal beside it (Modal:Menu). Desktop, Tablet and Mobile (Mobile has the Menu frame only).
- 07 SIGN-IN PANEL: one column of six (Default, Focus, Validation missing email, Validation invalid format, Loading, Success, email accepted). On Tablet there is a second journey 07.2 Sign-in page (landscape 16958:149 and portrait 16958:224), moved in from the old Tablet section 03 - SIGN-IN, which was then empty and deleted.
- 08 JOIN: 08.1 Email or mobile (24 screens, steps 1 to 5 down the column with their states below each step), 08.2 Continue with Google, 08.3 Continue with Apple.
- 10 COMMUNITY HOME: row 1 is journey 10.1 Home (Home, Loading, Error, a stack of Empty and the two carousels, then four overlay stacks: Notifications, Upcoming and Invitation; the six Ways call overlays and Invitation sent; Why we matched, Introduce us and Introduction sent; Arrange, Encourage, Message someone I trust and their confirmations). Below it five columns: 10.2 Messages (6), 10.3 Collaborations (2), 10.4 Care and support (3), 10.5 Settings (4), 10.6 Banter (1).
- 12 COLLABORATE: 12.1 Home (Collaborate Home and Empty), 12.2 Start a project (A1 to A8 down one column, A2 empty then ready, A6 then A6 error, Toast Draft saved beside A7, Toast Project saved beside A8), 12.3 Explore collaborators (B1 to B10 down one column, the B3 overlays, variants and toasts beside B3, B4, B5 and B9), 12.4 Saved and requests (B11, B11 empty, B12, B12 empty, Toast Removed from saved beside B11).
- 01, 11, 13, 14, 15: one page under the Flow Title.
Flow starting points: Desktop 28 (the 20 existing plus 08 JOIN (CONTINUE WITH GOOGLE), 08 JOIN (CONTINUE WITH APPLE), 10 COMMUNITY HOME (MESSAGES, COLLABORATIONS, CARE AND SUPPORT, SETTINGS, BANTER) and 12 COLLABORATE (SAVED AND REQUESTS)); Tablet 7 (01, 06 MENU, 07 SIGN-IN PANEL, 07 SIGN-IN PANEL (SIGN-IN PAGE), 08 JOIN (CREATE ACCOUNT), 08 JOIN (CONTINUE WITH GOOGLE), 08 JOIN (CONTINUE WITH APPLE)); Mobile 7 (01, 02, 06, 07, 08 and the two Join variants). Every existing entry was kept, renamed to the new section numbers on Tablet and Mobile. "06 - MENU" still starts on the Menu modal frame, and Desktop 02 has no starting point (its first item is a component set, which cannot be one; same as before).
Proof the prototype is intact: reaction counts and a hash of the sorted lines "nodeId, trigger, action, destination" match the 1 Nov baseline for every section except Desktop 07 (below). Tablet 07 matches as the sum of the old 03 (16 reactions, 73a318bb) and the old 04 (59 reactions, ce67a732). Other checks: 0 overlaps in all 24 sections, margins 500 px on all four sides, 1000 px between sections, every stroke bound to variable 10011:1179 (40 px, INSIDE, radius 80), every Flow Title status pending approval (yellow). Developer check: in every journey the first screen is directly under its Flow Title (300 px, 152 px for strips), the last screen of the main column is the outcome, no overlay sits in a step column, no duplicate frame names, no orphan frames.
Needs your decision, Desktop 07 SIGN-IN PANEL: the link fingerprint no longer matches the 1 Nov baseline (60 reactions both times). The 5 Loading frame (16996:3791) now moves after 1.8 s to Community Home 17095:5812; the baseline and the 1 Nov notes had it going to 6 Success (16996:3979), which then goes to Community Home after 1.5 s. It matched at my 1 Nov 19:13 check, no call of mine writes reactions, and the Tablet and Mobile panels are unchanged, so it was edited in the file afterwards. I did not touch it. If it was not intentional, point 5 Loading back at 6 Success.
Not linked (reported, not guessed): Join 1f Sending "Continue" and 2f Checking "Confirm and continue" (loading states, no click), 08.2 and 08.3 "Continue" (no backend), the 4 skeleton buttons on Home / Loading (Desktop 10). Same four Join buttons on Tablet and Mobile. Links that leave a section are the header, logo, nav and component state links of the signed in shell and the sign-in and menu entry points, as before.
Section sizes and x. Desktop: 01 x 0, 2512 x 17118; 02 x 3512, 4402 x 7775; 03 x 8914, 7948 x 6672; 04 x 17862, 2512 x 2355; 05 x 21374, 4324 x 16970; 06 x 26698, 6151 x 2550; 07 x 33849, 4595 x 8960; 08 x 39444, 7636 x 32036; 09 x 48080, 7948 x 2829; 10 x 57028, 15196 x 18987; 11 x 73224, 2512 x 2550; 12 x 76736, 13384 x 33580; 13 x 91120, 14 x 94632, 15 x 98144 (each 2512 x 2550). Tablet: 01 x 0, 2194 x 14261; 06 x 3194, 5515 x 2402; 07 x 9709, 5592 x 8432; 08 x 16301, 6682 x 28484. Mobile: 01 x 0, 2180 x 16440; 02 x 3180, 2180 x 2240; 06 x 6360, 2882 x 2442; 07 x 10242, 3880 x 8312; 08 x 15122, 6640 x 29444.
Decisions made without asking (the updated skill answered the earlier open questions): Join as three journeys (email or mobile, Google, Apple); Sign-in panel as a single column; Menu context beside the Menu modal; Community Home split into a hub row plus five journeys; Collaborate split into a hub plus three journeys; Tablet and Mobile renumbered to the Desktop numbers; the old Tablet full page sign-in merged as 07.2; plain page families kept as legacy strips. Moving a screen between columns or renaming a frame is safe for links, so any of these can change.
Tooling notes: (129) One atomic use_figma call per page with a config of bands, journeys, rows and cells (a cell is a node id, null or a stack of ids) lays out every section and throws when a listed id is not a child, a child is left unplaced or a name repeats, so nothing is left half done. The engine is in the scratchpad file engine.js. (130) `page.flowStartingPoints` replaces the whole list and each entry must be a top level FRAME, a component set is refused ("Can only set flow starting point that is a top level frame and a valid prototype source"). (131) A cloned Flow Title must be added to the placed set and appended with `section.appendChild(clone)`, then set its three text properties after loading the fonts of its text nodes. (132) A list of section fingerprints taken before is the proof that nothing was edited: compare the combined sorted lines when sections merge.

Tooling notes: (133) `node.clone()` of a child here placed the clone at page level, so always `parent.appendChild(clone)` and re-check the child count. (134) Reactions on an instance include inherited ones, so before rewriting an instance's reactions compare them with `mainComponent.reactions` and skip when equal. (135) Component sets move across pages with `frame.appendChild(set)` and keep their ids, variants and instances; read them while the source page is current. (136) A section holding only a source image strip stays valid as a one-row legacy strip (title width equals the strip).

Tooling notes: (137) The fingerprint format that reproduces every baseline is: every NODE action (including those nested inside CONDITIONAL blocks, 8 in section 08), line `nodeId|trigger|navigation:destinationId`, sorted, djb2, written `count:hash`. Without the nested ones section 08 reads 134 instead of 136. (138) Cloning a component variant and appending it to the set loses the reactions on its inner frames and the text property references (Title, Body): set `componentPropertyReferences` and `setReactionsAsync` again on the clone. (139) A reaction set on a layer of a component (Toast Undo, CLOSE) is inherited by every instance, including nested layers, so one edit wires all toasts. (140) Frame names and positions for inserted rows: shift every frame whose old y is at or below the insertion row, then resize the section to the lowest bottom plus 500.

### My Projects PRD received, expected flows and screens proposed, 3 Nov 2026 (12:00 question, 12:24 PRD), approved at 12:28 for the components, 12.10 and 12.11 (built, see the next subsection); 12.12 to 12.21 still awaiting approval
User asked (12:00): "What are the expected flows and screens for My Project page?" I proposed a first outline (list, drafts, studio, requests, manage, joined view). At 12:24 the user attached the real PRD ("AKINES, MY PROJECT, Product Requirements Document, Project Workspace and Collaboration Management", copy kept at /home/claude/akines-my-project-prd.md) and said "now update the flows and screens needed". The PRD replaces the first outline.
PRD summary: My Project is a workspace that only appears after a project is opened. Ten sections in left navigation: Overview, Studio, Conversations, Tasks, Files, Decisions, Team, Agreements, Reflection, Launch. Global project header always visible (left: project name, phase, project switcher; centre: team pulse, next milestone; right: notifications, Create AI, project settings). Three-column layout: left project navigation, centre current work, right human context panel that adapts to the section (availability, recent conversation, upcoming session, workload signals, unresolved decisions, private check-in entry). Supporting: shared project brief (10 parts, versioned, editable, significant changes need acknowledgement), contribution history (no presence metrics), relationship and team health (private by default, aggregated safe themes only), Create AI (cannot make ownership decisions, remove members, change agreements, send invitations or publish without approval; AI content must be distinguishable), notifications (10 types), empty states for every section (copy in PRD section 20), responsive rules (Tablet drawer, Mobile single column), sample project "Career Guidance for Everyone", phase Building, team pulse Strong alignment, next milestone Define the first student experience.
Proposed journeys, all inside section 12 - COLLABORATE, Desktop only, next free journey 12.10 (x 31304):
- 12.10 My projects list (entry, not in the PRD): Active, Drafts, Empty, Loading, Could not load, Modal: Delete draft, Toast: Draft deleted.
- 12.11 Overview: Overview, Empty (Your project is ready for its next step, Open Studio), Shared project brief (10 parts, suggest change, version history), Milestone detail.
- 12.12 Studio: Studio, Empty (A blank space for something meaningful), Modal: Create new work, Work detail and review, Shared canvas.
- 12.13 Conversations: list plus active conversation, Empty, Modal: Start conversation.
- 12.14 Tasks: List view, Board view, Milestone view (stacked), Empty, Create task, Task detail, Filter dropdown.
- 12.15 Files: File list with categories, Empty, Modal: Upload file, File preview.
- 12.16 Decisions: List, Empty, Propose decision, Decision detail, Modal: Acknowledge decision plus toast, Decision history.
- 12.17 Team: Team members, Empty (Every project starts with someone), Modal: Invite collaborator plus toast, Member detail with contribution history, Team availability. Owner-side interest requests (Accept, Not now) from D3 are NOT in the PRD; proposed here as an addition.
- 12.18 Agreements: Overview with six stages (Intent, Roles, Contributions, Ownership, Revenue, Formal), Empty, Stage detail, Agreement detail with versions and acknowledgements, Suggest change, Formal agreement stage (not automatically legally binding).
- 12.19 Reflection: Not available yet, Overview, Private check-in (six questions, who can see answers), Submitted, Team summary (aggregated), Suggested improvements (optional).
- 12.20 Launch: Not ready, Readiness, Materials, Team recognition and attribution, Modal: Confirm share (explicit approval), Toast: Project shared.
- 12.21 Project header overlays: Create AI panel, Notifications, Project switcher, Project settings (edit details, open or closed to collaborators, archive), Modal: Leave project.

Tooling notes: (155) findOne by name can return a frame that shares a name with a text layer; restrict to type TEXT before binding a text property. (156) strokeWeight can be mixed (a symbol); read per-side weights only after `'strokeTopWeight' in n && typeof === 'number'`. (157) layoutSizingHorizontal FILL works only after the node is appended to an auto-layout parent. (158) Cloned frames keep the old browser tab title and URL; set both on every clone. (159) `clone()` of a frame that sits on the page puts the clone on the PAGE, not in the section; appendChild into the section and set x, y again. A clone of a starting point frame also copies the starting point name; rebuild page.flowStartingPoints and drop the clone ids. (160) Assigning an unknown property on a node (node.__info) throws and rolls the call back; keep scratch data in variables. (161) The "Tab / My Projects" overrides inside every Header shell are ON_CLICK reactions on nested instances: read, change destinationId, write back with setReactionsAsync and keep the hover and CHANGE_TO entries. (162) For a whole-card click use setClick on the instance: it drops only ON_CLICK and keeps the hover and press entries. (163) Skeleton instances need the native 680 x 634 aspect or they overflow.

Tooling notes: (164) A component set clone keeps the same property keys as the original, so setProps by name still works; remap sibling-variant reaction destinations after cloning a set (map by name). (165) A cloned variant sits outside the set until `set.appendChild(variant)`; then lay the new variants out and resize the set. (166) generate_image with no nodeId creates a new image-filled frame; copy its imageHash onto the target layers and remove the frame. (167) A variant that must hug its content needs primaryAxisSizingMode AUTO set on every variant after resizing. (168) New frames cloned from a screen land on the PAGE, not in the section: appendChild them into the section and set x, y again.

Tooling notes: (169) A component property reference overwrites the node text with the property default in every variant, so a variant that needs different text (Composer Filled) needs its own property. (170) A child with layoutGrow 1 inside a HUG parent freezes at its previous height; set layoutGrow 0 and HUG on content columns so the card grows with its content. (171) `clone()` of a node inside a component variant lands on the PAGE; appendChild it into the variant afterwards and set layoutSizingHorizontal. (172) After switching an instance variant (Section or Context), nested reactions can survive by position (the decision link inherited the Review work click); re-read and clear them. (173) Border/Focus is a faint colour; the Line Field Focus state uses a 2 px Text/Primary stroke and a caret, so new fields should copy that. (174) Auto layout counts an inside stroke in the size, so Focus variants are 3 px taller than Default.

Tooling notes: (175) `figma.createComponent()` has no `component` property to reset; frames created for components are plain frames. (176) A cloned frame that sat in a FILL parent keeps layoutSizingVertical FILL; inside a HUG section it collapses to its fixed height and overlaps the next block, so set HUG. (177) Cloning a frame that is a flow starting point clones the starting point with the same name; rename or replace the list afterwards. (178) `setReactionsAsync` works on nested instance children (a link inside a header instance) and on an instance inside an instance; the array replaces everything, so repeat the hover and press entries. (179) Component properties cannot be bound to text inside a nested instance, so nested labels (Card Link, Chip) are edited with `setProperties` on the nested instance. (180) A flexible child needs `layoutGrow 1` (and FILL vertical) to push a footer to the bottom of a stretched card; rows then use FILL vertical on the card instances.
Code: see the subsection "Community feed in code" below.

### Community feed in code, 3 Nov 2026 (built about 18:20 Lagos, artifact version 10)
Built from `akines-landing/FEED_SPEC.md` plus the final Figma values (the Figma values win where they differ). Everything lives in `src/features/community/feed/`; only `src/features/community/routes.tsx` was edited outside it.
- Files: plain `.ts` for data (`arrangements.ts`, `content.ts` all copy, `dest.ts` every link target, `images.ts`, `useFeedVersion.ts`); `'use client'` components `FeedPage.tsx`, `Hero.tsx`, `Rail.tsx`, `MatchSlideshow.tsx`, `Posts.tsx`, `Sections.tsx`, `parts.tsx`; styles `feed.css` (prefix `fd-`, tunable geometry in the first block). 22 real PDF photos in `public/images/app/feed/`.

Match card is a 7 profile slideshow (dots plus counter, 44 px arrows, swipe at 40 px, arrow keys, Not now, Say hello with confirmation and toast), fixed card height.
- Figma values applied: hero Alice 48 on 2 lines max 760; rail circle rows 312 x 80; Banter title Alice 32 with pinned footer; DNA footer "Shared by 24 people"; product price Alice 24 and a 48 px Buy pill; post avatar 48 with a grey pill circle tag; reaction chips are the kit Chip at 44 px; wellbeing 984 x 440 with 592 text and 392 photo; state copy.

- Not matching Figma exactly: natural rail height is about 1136 px against 1174 in Figma (Trending is shorter, its inner spacing was not specified); the confirmation box and the buttons slot are both 62 px; the Banter card title steps down to 26 px at 1280 to 1479 and 24 px at 1023 and below so "collaboration" does not overflow.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `node scripts/build-preview.mjs <dir>` builds; Playwright 213 of 213 on the preview and on `next start` (section order A to D, rotation over 20 reloads never repeats, sticky rail at 1512 x 982, 1280 x 800, 1512 x 1300 and 1512 x 700, slideshow dots/arrows/swipe/keyboard/Not now/Say hello, geometry, link targets, states, reduced motion, no console errors or horizontal overflow at 10 widths from 360 to 2560); `node scripts/check-routes.mjs` 420 visits over 140 routes, no problems; `next build` 142 static pages. The only Next sweep flag is `/404` returning HTTP 404, which is the registered 404 screen. I re-ran tsc, the build, the route check and the Playwright suite myself after the worker finished and looked at A to D at 1512 and A at 402.
- Rebuild and republish: `cd akines-landing && npx tsc --noEmit && node scripts/build-preview.mjs <outDir>`; publish `<outDir>/artifact.html` to https://claude.ai/artifact/RjyEARNFhguXmvyzipTA1L with `root` = `<outDir>` and every file under `images/` in `files`. Playwright helpers are in the session scratchpad `feed-work/` (`verify.js` with `MODE=preview BASE=...`, `next-sweep.js`).
- Not done: no tablet or mobile Figma design for the feed (the code is responsive on its own, Figma is desktop only as agreed); Figma uses generated look-alike photos because the PDF photos cannot be uploaded from the shell.

### AI Assistant logo and the unified Button, 4 Nov 2026 (03:40 Lagos request with the logo image)
Request: (a) an AI assistant will sit at the bottom right corner of the whole website for signed-in and signed-out visitors; the supplied image (white arc head, two eyes, speech-bubble mouth on black) is its logo; make it an SVG and make colour combinations including an animated gradient, using only design system colours. (b) The landing page button with its hover effect should be one reusable component used everywhere in Akines, with states, sizes and types, in Figma (Components page) and in the code (artifact).
Decisions I made without asking: only the logo is delivered, the assistant launcher itself is not built; Figma gets the Button and Icon Button sets but existing screens are NOT swapped to them (needs approval); in code every button is migrated, so every app button now has the landing colour-swap hover.
Logo: traced from pixel measurements (IoU 0.99 against the supplied image). Geometry on a 600 grid: arc `M104.9 378.5A222.75 222.75 0 1 1 495.1 378.5` stroke 49.5, bubble `M300.0 577.5L431.2 489.8A155 66 0 1 0 300.0 520.3Z`, eyes (225, 258) and (376, 258) r 42.5. 17 SVGs in `public/brand/assistant/akines-assistant-<id>.svg` and the React component `src/components/AssistantMark.tsx`. Colourways: ink-on-ivory, paper-on-ink, ivory-on-ink, amber-on-ink, ink-on-amber, deep-amber-on-ivory, ink-on-amber-tint, duo-on-ivory, duo-on-ink, badge-ink, badge-amber, badge-paper, gradient-amber (static), animated-sheen, animated-badge-amber, animated-arc-ink (the animated ones use a 4.8 s CSS gradient flow with a `prefers-reduced-motion` stop). Contrast of the mark on its background is 3.8:1 or better in every set. Zip: `/home/claude/akines-assistant-logo-svgs.zip`. Specimen artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/LSdw2cNkKC17S5acSmL6mk (title "Akines Assistant Mark", private). Figma: component set "AI Assistant Logo" 17493:14620 (property Colourway, 16 variants, paints bound to variables) and "AI Assistant Mark / Mono" 17493:14621 in container 17493:14513 on the Components page.
Button (Figma, Components page, container 17494:14513 "Button system", 1402 x 3582, 400 below the logo block): component set "Button" 17498:15017, 216 variants. Properties: Style (Primary, Secondary, Accent, Ghost, Danger), Tone (Dark for light surfaces, Light for dark surfaces and photos; Danger has Dark only), Size (Small 36, Medium 48, Large 56, XL 62), State (Default, Hover, Pressed, Focus, Disabled, Loading), plus Label (text), Arrow (boolean), Arrow icon (swap), Left icon (boolean), Left icon swap. Text styles Button/Body/Small Bold (Small, Medium) and Button/Body/Bold (Large, XL). Layers: Fill with a Sweep rectangle (hidden below the pill, rises to cover it in Hover and Pressed), Ring, Focus inner (Border/Focus 2 px), Focus outer (ink 2 px), Left icon, Spinner (existing Spinner set), Label, Arrow with Icon. Every colour is a bound variable. Hover and press are prototype reactions on each Default variant: ON_HOVER to Hover with smart animate 0.55 s ease out, ON_PRESS to Pressed 0.1 s. Two backdrops sit behind the set (white for Dark tone, Akines/Primary/900 for Light tone) so both tones can be read.
Icon Button (container 17499:15017, y 86399): set "Icon Button" 17499:15828, 90 variants: Style Solid, Outline, Ghost; Tone Dark, Light; Size Small 36, Medium 44, Large 52; State Default, Hover, Pressed, Focus, Disabled; property Icon (swap, default arrow-up-right). Same sweep, reactions and backdrops.
Specimen: frame "Button specimen" 17500:15197 (y 88551): two panels (dark tone on ivory, light tone on dark) showing styles, sizes, Loading, Disabled, a left icon, a Hover frame and the icon buttons.
Colour map used in both sets (rest, hover sweep, hover label): Primary Dark ink, ivory sweep, ink with a 1 px ink ring. Primary Light ivory, ink sweep, inverse. Secondary Dark outline, ink sweep, ivory label. Secondary Light white outline, white sweep, ink label. Accent amber, ink sweep (Dark) or white sweep (Light). Ghost subtle grey sweep (Dark) or white at 16 percent (Light). Danger red outline, red sweep, inverse label. Pressed uses the next step (Surface/Muted, Action/Neutral/Hover, Subtle/Pressed).
Not in Figma: the Link style, the pressed scale of 0.97 (prototype reactions cannot scale a pill without distorting the label), the animated gradient (the logo animation is code and SVG only, Figma shows the static frames). Spec for the code: `akines-landing/BUTTON_SPEC.md`.
Tooling notes: (181) `combineAsVariants` clips nothing but the parent frame does: a container sized 2000 x 600 hid 5 sixths of the set in screenshots until it was resized to the set. (182) Variants with an absolutely positioned focus ring wider than the pill need the parent to have clipsContent off or the ring is cut. (183) `variable.resolveForConsumer(node).value` returns the real colour of an aliased variable, which `valuesByMode` does not. (184) `setReactionsAsync` with `ON_HOVER` and `ON_PRESS` CHANGE_TO on Default variants works for instances placed anywhere; do it in its own call so a failure does not roll back the build.
Code: the unified `Button` and `IconButton` are described in the next subsection once the migration is finished.

Tooling notes: (185) Setting `layoutMode` on a fresh frame makes it hug; set the sizing to FIXED and resize again (squashed icon circles). (186) `overlayPositionType`, `overlayBackground` and `overlayBackgroundInteraction` are read-only in the plugin, so an overlay is a viewport-sized transparent frame (centred by default) with an ON_CLICK CLOSE reaction. (187) Right after `clone()`, a nested instance may not be found by name until the clone was traversed once (`findAll(() => true)`), then the name search works. (188) A call that returns early without throwing does NOT roll back: check the container for stray clones after a failed attempt. (189) `setReactionsAsync` works on instances nested inside instances (the pill in 110 header copies, the rows in the menu). (190) Cloning a flow starting point clones the starting point too (duplicate names); rewrite `flowStartingPoints` after cloning. (191) Moving frames to another page works with `section.appendChild(frame)` on a section of the other page; reactions pointing back are dropped, clear them first on purpose. (192) CSS class names must be unique across features: two components used `fd-slide` and the later rule hid the card text. (193) The shell cannot reach mcp.figma.com (upload POST gives 403), so photos in Figma are generated or reuse existing image hashes.

Tooling notes: (194) `swapComponent` keeps the instance id and keeps label and boolean properties by matching name, but it resets cross-axis FILL to HUG and keeps a stale fixed height (52 stayed 52 until `layoutSizingVertical = 'HUG'`). The lost FILLs are found with `layoutAlign === 'STRETCH' && layoutSizingHorizontal === 'HUG'`; better, read the sizing before swapping and restore it. (195) After a swap the instance `reactions` hold both the new inherited hover and press and the old ones kept as overrides; rebuild as the non CHANGE_TO reactions plus the new Default variant's reactions. (196) A dry run that ends with `throw new Error(JSON.stringify(result))` returns the data and rolls everything back. (197) `setReactionsAsync` rejects a destination on another page, even for a reaction the node already carries. (198) `setPluginData` is not supported in this host; use `setSharedPluginData(namespace, key, value)`. (199) Cloning a flow starting point frame adds a starting point named the same for each clone; filter those out before adding unique ones, or the duplicate nodeIds error rolls the call back. (200) `layoutMode` on a component set gives a tidy variant grid; a raised dot needs a wrapper slot with MIN or MAX alignment, not absolute positioning.
Open: the Save Button choice, whether the launcher should sit on every signed-in screen, a Banter modal for the second result row, Tablet and Mobile versions of Create AI (you said Desktop only).

### Create AI starts a project inside the chat (Figma, Desktop), plain chat version, 4 Nov 2026, awaiting feedback
Request (12:18 Lagos): "1. Add that Create AI can start/create a project inside the pop up chat, by using same Start a Project flow." I asked two questions first. Answers: approach = "Conversation in the thread" (the other two options were step cards in the panel, and a panel that grows to a wider sheet), scope = "Figma first, code after approval". My first build copied the web flow too closely (progress bars, headlines, step cards, edit per section). At 12:44 Lagos you said: "The creation flow shouldn't look exactly like the creation flow we, it should be like a regular chat with the AI... and should look very simple". I rebuilt it as a plain chat and removed the heavy version. Only Figma changed. The code, the zip and artifact version 13 are unchanged. The next step after approval is the code (Remaining work 0zzzz).

What the chat looks like: the assistant writes one or two short plain sentences, your answers are normal bubbles on the right, choices are quick-reply chips under the question, the usual message field sits at the bottom (Default with the placeholder "Or type your own answer" on chip steps, Filled with the dummy answer on text steps), and the brief appears as one small outlined card. No progress bars, no headlines, no step cards.

Conversation in order (same questions, dummy answers and brief rules as src/features/collaborate/start):
1. You: "Start a project". Assistant: "Happy to help you start a project. I'll ask a few short questions, and you can skip any of them. What would you like to bring to life?" plus the 10 idea type chips (Idea type chips go to the next message).
2. You: "An application". Assistant: "Nice. Tell me about your idea in a few sentences." The field holds the dummy idea sentence.
3. Four questions, one message each, each with a small "Skip this one" button and the field prefilled with that question's dummy answer: "Thanks. What problem are you trying to solve?", "Who is this for?", "What would success look like?", "Why do you care about this?". The previous answer shows as your bubble.
4. "Where are you with this idea?" plus the 6 stage chips (Ready to plan is the dummy).
5. "What would help you move forward? Pick as many as you like." plus the 7 need chips (Collaborators and Research selected) and a Done button.
6. Preparing: "Thank you. Give me a moment while I put your brief together." plus typing dots, moves on after 3 s. Interrupted: "Sorry, something interrupted your brief. Your answers are saved. Shall I try again?" with Try again and Start over.
7. Review: "Here is your project brief." plus the Brief summary card (name, overview, Stage line, Support needed line), one plain suggestion sentence ("One idea: name the students, teachers and parents you want to help, so it is easier to find the right collaborators."), and three buttons: Approve brief, Change something, Save as draft.
8. Change something: "Sure. What would you like to change?" with the dummy edit "Add that we will start with ten schools in Lagos." Then "Done. I've added it to the overview." with the updated card (Kind Updated), Approve brief and Save as draft.
9. Approved: "Your brief is approved. People you contact can see the name, overview, stage and support needed. Everything else stays private. Would you like to look for collaborators now?" with Explore collaborators and Save project.
10. Save project: "Saved. Career Guidance for Everyone is now in My Projects." with an Open my project button. Save as draft: "Draft saved. You can finish it any time from My Projects." with an Open My Projects button.

Tooling notes: (201) An instance made from a variant component is named after the component SET, so find it with `mainComponent.parent.name`. (202) A thrown error rolls back a whole call, so every finder needs a null guard and a per node try and catch. (203) A hug width frame with a FILL width text plus `maxWidth = 300` gives a wrapping bubble. (204) `appendChild` moves components between frames and keeps ids and instances. (205) Reported heights right after `swapComponent` can be stale; take a screenshot. (206) The sandbox cannot download figma.com screenshot URLs; call get_screenshot with `enableBase64Response`. (207) `swapComponent` on the panel instance kept its id and its custom name but dropped every nested reaction, so I rewired by finding nested nodes by name (Close, Start again, Send, button labels) and the chips by `mainComponent.parent.name`. (208) To keep hover and press on a nested button, rebuild its reactions as the non ON_CLICK ones plus the new click. (209) A one line dummy answer in the Filled field uses `textTruncation = 'ENDING'` with `maxLines = 1`. (210) Before removing a component, `getInstancesAsync()` on each variant shows whether anything still uses it.
Open: should the chat also take the other Collaborate tasks (explore collaborators, open a project), should the quick reply chips lose the radio circle and become plain pills, should the launcher sit on the Collaborate screens, Tablet and Mobile versions.

## Workspace and Navigation update (Figma only), 4 Nov 2026

Source: the user's "Workspace & Navigation Update PRD" (attachment af573c2a). The three screenshots (Procreate, Adobe Fresco, a dark editor) are inspiration only. Nothing in code or the artifact has changed for this update; build it only after the user approves the Figma designs.

### What the PRD asks for
- Project navigation order: Overview, Tasks, Studio, Workspace, Conversations, Files, Decisions, Team, Agreements, Reflection, Launch (done earlier in Project Nav set 17352:14054).
- Two Workspace types only: Digital Art (Fresco and Procreate feel) and Research (Docs and Word feel). No other types, no full apps, no backend, no new roles, no other navigation or creation changes.
- The interface must show who has access, who is active, edit vs view only, who can comment, who manages access. It must not silently override permissions.
- Real-time states: working, viewing, commenting, saving, saved, collaborator lost connection, reconnecting, no edit permission.
- Create AI recommends one Workspace type during project creation. The user must tap "Accept Workspace" before the next step; the accepted type is saved to the project. The recommendation screen shows type, Workspace name, short why, preview and the accept action.
- Required screens: (1) nav with Workspace, (2) Digital Art Workspace, (3) Research Workspace, (4) Create AI recommendation, (5) accepted state, (6) real-time states.

### Tooling notes (new)
- (a) `clone()` of a node on another page puts the clone on the CURRENT page (Title Page). Always `section.appendChild(clone)` and set x and y afterwards.
- (b) NAVIGATE destinations must be top-level frames on the same page; this error also appears when the source node sits INSIDE the destination frame (a self link). Check the node's top-level frame before adding a link, and wrap each node in try and catch.
- (c) `setCurrentPageAsync` takes about 5.7 s and caused timeouts; edit by node id without switching pages where possible.
- (d) Hidden nodes inside instances (for example the Avatar Initials text) are skipped unless `figma.skipInvisibleInstanceChildren=false`.
- (e) "Service Unavailable" and "MCP server connection lost" leave nothing applied; split calls, build subtrees detached then append, and retry screenshots one at a time with a smaller `maxDimension` (1000 to 1400).
- (f) `resize()` resets sizing to FIXED; set FILL or HUG again after appending into an auto-layout parent.
- (g) Code, Next.js: the quick preview build (esbuild) does not catch server-side faults, so always run the production `next build` before publishing. A file with `'use client'` that exports data (paths, arrays) is unreadable on the server, and a route file with `'use client'` breaks `generateStaticParams`. Keep route files and path constants free of the directive.
- (h) Code: the Create AI panel opens by itself once a day, 1.6 s after the first page. In automated tests with a fresh browser it appears over whatever screen is under test; it is not caused by the screen's own buttons.

**Decisions I made (you can change any of them)**
- Explore arrows scroll the row to its start or end, like the Figma prototype.
- The rail is sticky under the header in code (that behaviour comes from Community Home; Figma does not show it). At 1280 and up the header is 132 px (bar plus tab row), so the rail and scroll targets stop below it.

**Deviations and notes**
- Text colour of the small orange tags and the event date is `--text-accent` #AE690A (Figma Accent/Akines/Secondary/Text, also used by the Community Home feed card tags). On the cream page it is about 3.8:1 at 12 px, under the 4.5:1 that WCAG 2.2 AA asks for. I kept the Figma value. A passing replacement is #955708 (5.1:1 on cream, 5.7:1 on white); changing `--text-accent` in `src/styles/app-tokens.css` fixes it everywhere, and Figma needs the same change in the variable.
- `Text/Secondary` is #6E6E6E in code (Figma #6F6F6F), already in the project for AA.
- The photos are AI look-alikes like the rest of the project.
- Tablet and Mobile are not designed. Code degrades sensibly (one column under 1100, cards stack, carousels scroll) but that is not a design.

- Focus: tabbed through the whole page; every control has a 2 px ring, photo cards and interest cards are ringed around the whole card, nothing is clipped.
- Route sweep (`scripts/check-routes.mjs`, three widths): 822 visits over 274 routes (every route at 1512, 1194 and 402), 4815 internal links checked on the desktop pass, no problems, no console errors, no horizontal overflow.

Version 23 lost 13 images (the 12 team portraits in `images/app/team/` and `images/app/meet/shared-screen.jpg`) because that publish listed only the 9 new Discover photos in `files`. Rule for every artifact publish: send EVERY file under `images/` in `files` (121 files now), not only the new ones. Version 24 does that and the live file list was checked (124 files with the old duplicates). `akines-landing.zip` rebuilt.

- Class collision: the older Community "discover" pages (`projects/`, `opportunities/`) used the same `cd-` prefix as the new dashboard (`cd-card`, `cd-card__title`, `cd-label`, `cd-member__name`, `cd-member__role`, `cd-portrait`, `cd-status`). Tooling notes (for later Figma work)
- `setReactionsAsync` replaces everything, so read `node.reactions`, drop ON_CLICK, push the new one and set the full list (keeps hover and press).
- NAVIGATE, OVERLAY and SWAP destinations must be top level frames. CHANGE_TO destinations are component variants.
- Removing children of an instance throws. Use `visible = false`. Hidden instance children vanish from `children` unless `figma.skipInvisibleInstanceChildren = false`, which also changes text index counts. Nodes inside instances can be fetched by id such as 'I17703:74261;17354:13979'.
- The rowOf helper returns the row of its first argument, so check which row you hide.
- `combineAsVariants` needs the components on the page, not inside an auto layout parent. Call `set.resizeWithoutConstraints(w, h)` after moving variants. Add component properties after combining, then set `componentPropertyReferences`.
- `clone()` lands on the page or the same parent, so use `section.appendChild` and set x and y again.
- Screenshots need `enableBase64Response: true` in this sandbox.

## Governance from the blueprint and PRD
Only describe functionality that exists or label it a concept preview. Do not invent testimonials, member counts or matching results. Avoid guarantees about compatibility, safety or verification. Every CTA must lead to a valid destination. Meet WCAG 2.2 AA contrast, respect reduced motion, no scroll-jacking.

## Observations about the current live page
- Rhythm steps 3 and 4 (Make, Reflect) share identical description text.
- Almost every CTA points to the login page.
- Only the KinCircle experience is described in detail.
